int(hidden_size * expert_width_multiplier)  # hidden_size // 2\\n        lm_expert_config.intermediate_size = get_intermediate_size(int(hidden_size * expert_width_multiplier))\\n        lm_expert_config.num_hidden_layers = self.num_vlm_layers\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"

### Assistant

_source line 440_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
I see. When `load_vlm_weights=True`:
- Downloads the full VLM from HuggingFace
- Gets config from the downloaded model
- Downloads the processor

When `load_vlm_weights=False`:
- Downloads only the config from HuggingFace (smaller)
- Creates the model from config (random weights)
- Downloads the processor

In both cases, it still needs to download from HuggingFace (config + processor at minimum). But downloading config + processor is much faster than the full model.

The key insight: after `SmolVLAPolicy.from_pretrained(pretrained_dir)`, the policy loads the architecture and then loads the weights from model.safetensors. 

So setting `load_vlm_weights: false` means the VLM weights from model.safetensors will override whatever was initialized from HuggingFace anyway. This way I can skip downloading the large model files entirely while still getting the correct weights loaded from the local safetensors file.

The config and processor files are tiny, so even with the HF_ENDPOINT pointing to hf-mirror.com, they'll download quickly. Now I need to update the config.json in the pretrained directory.
```

</details>

It hit line 74, which is the `if not is_libero_plus` branch (the standard LIBERO path). So `is_libero_plus=False`. The task filename includes `_table_1` which is the issue. The `init_states_file` attribute of the task has `_table_1` in it, meaning the LIBERO benchmark on westd-38929 is returning task descriptions with filenames that include `_table_1`, which shouldn't be there for standard LIBERO tasks.

Let me check what version of the LIBERO library is being used.

t0)\\n\\n    tokenizer = get_tokenizer(policy)\\n\\n    # Load normalization stats\\n    norm_stats = load_norm_stats(args.pretrained_dir, device)\\n    log.info(\\\"Loaded normalization stats: state_mean=%s, action_mean=%s\\\",\\n             norm_stats[\\\"state_mean\\\"].tolist()[:3], norm_stats[\\\"action_mean\\\"].tolist()[:3])\\n\\n    # Detect camera keys from model config\\n    camera_keys = [\\n        k for k, v in policy.config.input_features.items()\\n        if v.type.name == \\\"VISUAL\\\"\\n    ]\\n    log.info(\\\"Camera keys from config: %s\\\", camera_keys)\\n\\n    connector = None\\n    if args.mode == \\\"random_prune\\\":\\n        connector = find_connector(policy.model)\\n        if connector is None:\\n            raise RuntimeError(\\\"Cannot find connector module in model\\\")\\n\\n    # Load LIBERO\\n    from libero.libero import benchmark\\n    from lerobot.envs.libero import get_task_init_states, LiberoEnv\\n\\n    bench_dict = benchmark.get_benchmark_dict()\\n    if args.suite not in bench_dict:\\n        raise ValueError(f\\\"Unknown suite '{args.suite}'. Available: {sorted(bench_dict.keys())}\\\")\\n    task_suite = bench_dict[args.suite]()\\n    n_tasks = len(task_suite.tasks)\\n    task_ids = list(range(n_tasks))\\n    if args.task_ids:\\n        task_ids = [int(x) for x in args.task_ids.split(\\\",\\\")]\\n\\n    log.info(\\\"Suite '%s': %d tasks, evaluating %s\\\", args.suite, n_tasks, task_ids)\\n\\n    all_task_results = {}\\n    total_successes = 0\\n    total_episodes = 0\\n\\n    for task_id in task_ids:\\n        task = task_suite.get_task(task_id)\\n        task_desc = task.language\\n        log.info(\\\"--- Task %d: %s ---\\\", task_id, task_desc)\\n\\n        env = LiberoEnv(\\n            task_suite=task_suite,\\n            task_id=task_id,\\n            task_suite_name=args.suite,\\n            camera_name=\\\"agentview_image,robot0_eye_in_hand_image\\\",\\n            obs_type=\\\"pixels_agent_pos\\\",\\n            observation_width=256,\\n            observation_height=256,\\n            init_states=True,\\n            n_envs=args.num_episodes,\\n            episode_index=0,\\n            control_mode=\\\"relative\\\",\\n        )\\n\\n        task_successes = []\\n        task_lengths = []\\n\\n        for ep in range(args.num_episodes):\\n            obs, info = env.reset(seed=args.seed + task_id * 1000 + ep)\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"

The fix: add `is_libero_plus=True` to the `LiberoEnv` constructor call. But wait, this is the standard LIBERO benchmark, not LIBERO-Plus. The issue is that the LIBERO Python package installed on westd-38929 is the LIBERO-Plus version, which adds `_table_1` suffixes to standard task names.


Let me look at what the `is_libero_plus` parameter actually controls in the constructor to understand the full scope of this mismatch.

Yet another error! The model loaded successfully, but now the environment is crashing because the LIBERO-Plus `_table_1` variant needs a specific arena XML file that doesn't exist. 

The task name is `pick_up_the_black_bowl_between_the_plate_and_the_ramekin_and_place_it_on_the_plate_table_1`, which is a LIBERO-Plus perturbation that uses a different table texture (`Cobblestone01_GLOSS_6K`). The LIBERO-Plus package expects this asset file, but it's not there.
 
I'm realizing the core problem: setting `is_libero_plus=True` doesn't just change how init states load—it also makes the environment load LIBERO-Plus variants with different visual properties like table textures and camera views. The LIBERO library installed on this machine is LIBERO-Plus, which adds these perturbation variants to the standard task suite. I need to either install the original LIBERO library instead, or figure out how to use the standard tasks without triggering the LIBERO-Plus variants.

Looking at how X-VLA handles this—they use their own eval script with a custom environment setup that successfully runs `libero_object` tasks, so I should check their approach to see how they avoid this issue.
```

### Assistant

_source line 570_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
The X-VLA eval uses `benchmark.get_benchmark(args.suite)()` and `task_suite.get_task_init_states(task_id)` directly (from LIBERO's own API), not through lerobot's `LiberoEnv`. And it creates `OffScreenRenderEnv` directly, not `LiberoEnv`.

The key differences:
1. X-VLA uses `benchmark.get_benchmark(args.suite)()` (original LIBERO API)
2. SmolVLA/lerobot uses `benchmark.get_benchmark_dict()[suite]()` with `LiberoEnv`

2. Key Technical Concepts:

   - LeRobot framework: SmolVLAPolicy.from_pretrained() loads model from checkpoint dir
   - LIBERO benchmark: Robot manipulation eval suite (spatial, object, goal, etc.)
   - LIBERO-Plus: Extended LIBERO with perturbation variants (adds `_table_1`, `_view_1` etc. suffixes to tasks)
   - VLM architecture: SmolVLM2-500M-Instruct loaded via AutoModelForImageTextToText
   - `load_vlm_weights` config: When False, only instantiates architecture (random weights), then `from_pretrained` overrides from model.safetensors

mean) / (std + eps).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    return (state_tensor - norm_stats[\\\"state_mean\\\"]) / (norm_stats[\\\"state_std\\\"] + norm_stats[\\\"eps\\\"])\\n\\n\\ndef unnormalize_actions(actions, norm_stats):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Unnormalize predicted actions: x * std + mean.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    return actions * norm_stats[\\\"action_std\\\"] + norm_stats[\\\"action_mean\\\"]\\n\\n\\ndef parse_args():\\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\\\"Eval official SmolVLA on LIBERO\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--pretrained_dir\\\", type=str, required=True,\\n                   help=\\\"Path to official lerobot-format checkpoint dir\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--suite\\\", type=str, default=\\\"libero_object\\\",\\n                   choices=list(SUITE_MAX_STEPS.keys()))\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--num_episodes\\\", type=int, default=50)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--gpu\\\", type=int, default=0)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--seed\\\", type=int, default=42)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--action_horizon\\\", type=int, default=50)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--max_steps\\\", type=int, default=None)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--output\\\", type=str, default=None)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--mode\\\", choices=[\\\"baseline\\\", \\\"random_prune\\\"], default=\\\"baseline\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--pruning_method\\\", type=str, default=\\\"true_removal\\\",\\n                   choices=[\\\"zero_mask\\\", \\\"true_removal\\\"])\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--k_ratio\\\", type=float, default=0.9,\\n                   help=\\\"Fraction of visual tokens to KEEP (0.9 = keep 90%%)\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--task_ids\\\", type=str, default=None)\\n    return p.parse_args()\\n\\n\\n# --- State preprocessing ---\\n\\ndef quat_to_axis_angle(quat: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:\\n    q = quat.astype(np.float64)\\n    w = np.clip(q[3], -1.0, 1.0)\\n    den = np.sqrt(max(1.0 - w * w, 0.0))\\n    if den > 1e-10:\\n        angle = 2.0 * np.arccos(w)\\n        axis = q[:3] / den\\n        return (axis * angle).astype(np.float32)\\n    return np.zeros(3, dtype=np.float32)\\n\\n\\ndef extract_state(obs: dict) -> np.ndarray:\\n    rs = obs[\\\"robot_state\\\"]\\n    eef_pos = np.asarray(rs[\\\"eef\\\"][\\\"pos\\\"], dtype=np.float32)\\n    eef_quat = np.asarray(rs[\\\"eef\\\"][\\\"quat\\\"], dtype=np.float64)\\n    gripper_qpos = np.asarray(rs[\\\"gripper\\\"][\\\"qpos\\\"], dtype=np.float32)\\n    eef_aa = quat_to_axis_angle(eef_quat)\\n    return np.concatenate([eef_pos, eef_aa, gripper_qpos])\\n\\n\\ndef obs_to_batch(obs, task_text, tokenizer, camera_keys, device, norm_stats=None):\\n    batch = {}\\n    for cam_key in camera_keys:\\n        short_name = cam_key.split(\\\".\\\")[-1]\\n        img = obs[\\\"pixels\\\"][short_nam\\n\\n... {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"X-VLA Vision Token Pruning Evaluation on LIBERO.\\n\\nPrunes vision tokens by L2 norm after Florence2's image encoder + linear projection,\\nbefore they enter the Florence2 language encoder.  - 20D proprio: [pos(3)+rot6d(6)+0.0] + zeros(10)\\n  - domain_id=3 for LIBERO\\n  - Closed-loop proprio update from last predicted action\\n\\nUsage:\\n    CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 MUJOCO_GL=egl python numpy as np\\nimport torch\\nfrom PIL import Image\\n\\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))\\n\\nlogging.basicConfig(\\n    level=logging.INFO,\\n    format=\\\"%(asctime)s \\\"libero_object\\\": 800,\\n    \\\"libero_goal\\\": 800,\\n    \\\"libero_10\\\": 900,\\n    \\\"libero_90\\\": 800,\\n}\\n\\nEPS = 1e-6\\n\\n\\ndef parse_args():\\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\\\"X-VLA pruning eval on LIBERO\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--model_path\\\", type=str, default=\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/X-VLA-Libero\\\",\\n                   help=\\\"HuggingFace model path or local dir\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--suite\\\", type=str, default=\\\"libero_object\\\",\\n                   choices=list(SUITE_MAX_STEPS.keys()))\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--keep_ratio\\\", type=float, default=1.0,\\n                   help=\\\"Fraction of vision tokens to keep (1.0 = no pruning)\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--num_episodes\\\", type=int, default=50)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--gpu\\\", type=int, default=0)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--seed\\\", type=int, default=42)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--max_steps\\\", type=int, default=None)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--output\\\", type=str, default=None)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--task_ids\\\", type=str, default=None)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--domain_id\\\", type=int, default=3)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--denoise_steps\\\", type=int, default=10,\\n                   help=\\\"Flow matching denoising steps\\\")\\n    return p.parse_args()\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Pruning hook\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef install_vision_pruning(model, keep_ratio: float):\\n    if keep_ratio >= 1.0:\\n        log.info(\\\"keep_ratio=%.2f, no pruning applied\\\", keep_ratio)\\n        return\\n\\n    original_encode = model.vlm._encode_image\\n\\n    @wraps(original_encode)\\n    def pruned_encode(pixel_values):\\n        features = original_encode(pixel_values)\\n        N = features.shape[1]\\n        k = max(1, int(keep_ratio * N))\\n        if k >= N:\\n            return features\\n        norms = features.norm(dim=-1)\\n        _, indices = norms.topk(k, dim=-1)\\n        indices_sorted = indices.sort(dim=-1).values\\n        pruned = features.gather(\\n            1, indices_sorted.unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, features.shape[-1])\\n        )\\n        return pruned\\n\\n    model.vlm._encode_image = pruned_encode\\n    log.info(\\\"Vision pruning installed: keep_ratio=%.2f\\\", keep_ratio)\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Model loading\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef load_model(model_path: str, device: torch.device):\\n    from transformers import AutoModel, AutoProcessor\\n\\n    log.info(\\\"Loading X-VLA from %s\\\", model_path)\\n    model = AutoModel.from_pretrained(\\n        model_path,\\n        torch_dtype=torch.float32,\\n        trust_remote_code=True,\\n        low_cpu_mem_usage=True,\\n    )\\n    model = model.to(device).eval()\\n    processor = AutoProcessor.from_pretrained(model_path, trust_remote_code=True)\\n\\n    n_params = sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters())\\n    log.info(\\\"Model loaded: %.1fM params, dtype=%s\\\", n_params / 1e6, model.dtype)\\n    return model, processor\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# 6D rotation utilities (following X-VLA's convention)\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef mat_to_rot6d(R: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:\\n    return np.concatenate([R[:3, 0], R[:3, 1]], axis=-1).astype(np.float32)\\n\\n\\ndef rot6d_to_axis_angle(r6d: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:\\n    import robosuite.utils.transform_utils as T\\n\\n    single = r6d.ndim == 1\\n    if single:\\n        r6d = r6d[None, :]\\n\\n    a1 = r6d[:, 0:3]\\n    a2 = r6d[:, 3:6]\\n\\n    b1 = a1 / (np.linalg.norm(a1, axis=-1, keepdims=True) + EPS)\\n    dot_prod = np.sum(b1 * a2, axis=-1, keepdims=True)\\n    b2_orth = a2 - dot_prod * b1\\n    b2 = b2_orth / (np.linalg.norm(b2_orth, axis=-1, keepdims=True) + EPS)\\n    b3 = np.cross(b1, b2, axis=-1)\\n\\n    R = np.stack([b1, b2, b3], axis=-1)\\n\\n    results = []\\n    for i in range(R.shape[0]):\\n        quat = T.mat2quat(R[i])\\n        aa = T.quat2axisangle(quat)\\n        results.append(aa)\\n\\n    result = np.stack(results, axis=0)\\n    return result[0] if single else result\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Observation processing\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef extract_proprio_20d(env) -> np.ndarray:\\n    robot = env.env.robots[0]\\n    ee_pos = robot.controller.ee_pos.copy()\\n    ee_ori_mat = robot.controller.ee_ori_mat.copy()\\n    ee_rot6d = mat_to_rot6d(ee_ori_mat)\\n\\n    arm1 = np.concatenate([ee_pos, ee_rot6d, np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float32)])\\n    arm2 = np.zeros(10, dtype=np.float32)\\n    return np.concatenate([arm1, arm2])\\n\\n\\ndef decode_action_20d_to_7d(action_20d: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:\\n    single = action_20d.ndim == 1\\n    if single:\\n        action_20d = action_20d[None, :]\\n\\n    pos = action_20d[:, :3]\\n    rot6d = action_20d[:, 3:9]\\n    grip = action_20d[:, 9:10]\\n\\n    axis_angle = rot6d_to_axis_angle(rot6d)\\n    grip_disc = np.where(grip > 0.5, 1.0, -1.0)\\n\\n    result = np.concatenate([pos, axis_angle, grip_disc], axis=-1).astype(np.float32)\\n    return result[0] if single else result\\n\\n\\ndef get_images_from_obs(obs) -> list:\\n    images = []\\n    for cam_name in [\\\"agentview_image\\\", \\\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\\\"]:\\n        img = obs.get(cam_name, None)\\n        if img is None:\\n            for k, v in obs.items():\\n                if cam_name in k and isinstance(v, np.ndarray):\\n                    img = v\\n                    break\\n        if img is not None:\\n            if img.dtype in (np.float32, np.float64):\\n                img = (np.clip(img, 0, 1) * 255).astype(np.uint8)\\n            if cam_name == \\\"agentview_image\\\":\\n                img = np.flip(np.flip(img, 0), 1).copy()\\n            images.append(Image.fromarray(img))\\n    return images\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Action prediction\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\n@torch.no_grad()\\ndef predict_actions(model, processor, images, task_desc, proprio_20d, domain_id,\\n                    device, denoise_steps=10):\\n    inputs = processor(images=images, language_instruction=task_desc)\\n\\n    proprio_t = torch.tensor(proprio_20d, dtype=torch.float32).unsqueeze(0).to(device)\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"}

json\\nimport logging\\nimport os\\nimport sys\\nimport time as time_mod\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\nfrom functools import wraps\\n\\nos.environ.setdefault(\\\"MUJOCO_GL\\\", \\\"egl\\\")\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport torch\\nfrom PIL import Image\\n\\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))\\n\\nlogging.basicConfig(\\n    level=logging.INFO,\\n    format=\\\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s\\\",\\n    datefmt=\\\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\\\",\\n)\\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\nSUITE_MAX_STEPS = {\\n    \\\"libero_spatial\\\": 800,\\n    \\\"libero_object\\\": 800,\\n    \\\"libero_goal\\\": 800,\\n    \\\"libero_10\\\": 900,\\n    \\\"libero_90\\\": 800,\\n}\\n\\nEPS = 1e-6\\n\\n\\ndef parse_args():\\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\\\"X-VLA pruning eval on LIBERO\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--model_path\\\", type=str, default=\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/X-VLA-Libero\\\",\\n                   help=\\\"HuggingFace model path or local dir\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--suite\\\", type=str, default=\\\"libero_object\\\",\\n                   choices=list(SUITE_MAX_STEPS.keys()))\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--keep_ratio\\\", type=float, default=1.0,\\n                   help=\\\"Fraction of vision tokens to keep (1.0 = no pruning)\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--num_episodes\\\", type=int, default=50)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--gpu\\\", type=int, default=0)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--seed\\\", type=int, default=42)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--max_steps\\\", type=int, default=None)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--output\\\", type=str, default=None)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--task_ids\\\", type=str, default=None)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--domain_id\\\", type=int, default=3)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--denoise_steps\\\", type=int, default=10,\\n                   help=\\\"Flow matching denoising steps\\\")\\n    return p.parse_args()\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Pruning hook\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef install_vision_pruning(model, keep_ratio: float):\\n    if keep_ratio >= 1.0:\\n        log.info(\\\"keep_ratio=%.2f, no pruning applied\\\", keep_ratio)\\n        return\\n\\n    original_encode = model.vlm._encode_image\\n\\n    @wraps(original_encode)\\n    def pruned_encode(pixel_values):\\n        features = original_encode(pixel_values)\\n        N = features.shape[1]\\n        k = max(1, int(keep_ratio * N))\\n        if k >= N:\\n            return features\\n        norms = features.norm(dim=-1)\\n        _, indices = norms.topk(k, dim=-1)\\n        indices_sorted = indices.sort(dim=-1).values\\n        pruned = features.gather(\\n            1, indices_sorted.unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, features.shape[-1])\\n        )\\n        return pruned\\n\\n    model.vlm._encode_image = pruned_encode\\n    log.info(\\\"Vision pruning installed: keep_ratio=%.2f\\\", keep_ratio)\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Model loading\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef load_model(model_path: str, device: torch.device):\\n    from transformers import AutoModel, AutoProcessor\\n\\n    log.info(\\\"Loading X-VLA from %s\\\", model_path)\\n    model = AutoModel.from_pretrained(\\n        model_path,\\n        torch_dtype=torch.float32,\\n        trust_remote_code=True,\\n        low_cpu_mem_usage=True,\\n    )\\n    model = model.to(device).eval()\\n    processor = AutoProcessor.from_pretrained(model_path, trust_remote_code=True)\\n\\n    n_params = sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters())\\n    log.info(\\\"Model loaded: %.1fM params, dtype=%s\\\", n_params / 1e6, model.dtype)\\n    return model, processor\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# 6D rotation utilities (following X-VLA's convention)\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef mat_to_rot6d(R: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:\\n    return np.concatenate([R[:3, 0], R[:3, 1]], axis=-1).astype(np.float32)\\n\\n\\ndef rot6d_to_axis_angle(r6d: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:\\n    import robosuite.utils.transform_utils as T\\n\\n    single = r6d.ndim == 1\\n    if single:\\n        r6d = r6d[None, :]\\n\\n    a1 = r6d[:, 0:3]\\n    a2 = r6d[:, 3:6]\\n\\n    b1 = a1 / (np.linalg.norm(a1, axis=-1, keepdims=True) + EPS)\\n    dot_prod = np.sum(b1 * a2, axis=-1, keepdims=True)\\n    b2_orth = a2 - dot_prod * b1\\n    b2 = b2_orth / (np.linalg.norm(b2_orth, axis=-1, keepdims=True) + EPS)\\n    b3 = np.cross(b1, b2, axis=-1)\\n\\n    R = np.stack([b1, b2, b3], axis=-1)\\n\\n    results = []\\n    for i in range(R.shape[0]):\\n        quat = T.mat2quat(R[i])\\n        aa = T.quat2axisangle(quat)\\n        results.append(aa)\\n\\n    result = np.stack(results, axis=0)\\n    return result[0] if single else result\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Observation processing\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef extract_proprio_20d(env) -> np.ndarray:\\n    robot = env.env.robots[0]\\n    ee_pos = robot.controller.ee_pos.copy()\\n    ee_ori_mat = robot.controller.ee_ori_mat.copy()\\n    ee_rot6d = mat_to_rot6d(ee_ori_mat)\\n\\n    arm1 = np.concatenate([ee_pos, ee_rot6d, np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float32)])\\n    arm2 = np.zeros(10, dtype=np.float32)\\n    return np.concatenate([arm1, arm2])\\n\\n\\ndef decode_action_20d_to_7d(action_20d: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:\\n    single = action_20d.ndim == 1\\n    if single:\\n        action_20d = action_20d[None, :]\\n\\n    pos = action_20d[:, :3]\\n    rot6d = action_20d[:, 3:9]\\n    grip = action_20d[:, 9:10]\\n\\n    axis_angle = rot6d_to_axis_angle(rot6d)\\n    grip_disc = np.where(grip > 0.5, 1.0, -1.0)\\n\\n    result = np.concatenate([pos, axis_angle, grip_disc], axis=-1).astype(np.float32)\\n    return result[0] if single else result\\n\\n\\ndef get_images_from_obs(obs) -> list:\\n    images = []\\n    for cam_name in [\\\"agentview_image\\\", \\\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\\\"]:\\n        img = obs.get(cam_name, None)\\n        if img is None:\\n            for k, v in obs.items():\\n                if cam_name in k and isinstance(v, np.ndarray):\\n                    img = v\\n                    break\\n        if img is not None:\\n            if img.dtype in (np.float32, np.float64):\\n                img = (np.clip(img, 0, 1) * 255).astype(np.uint8)\\n            if cam_name == \\\"agentview_image\\\":\\n                img = np.flip(np.flip(img, 0), 1).copy()\\n            images.append(Image.fromarray(img))\\n    return images\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Action prediction\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\n@torch.no_grad()\\ndef predict_actions(model, processor, images, task_desc, proprio_20d, domain_id,\\n                    device, denoise_steps=10):\\n    inputs = processor(images=images, language_instruction=task_desc)\\n\\n    proprio_t = torch.tensor(proprio_20d, dtype=torch.float32).unsqueeze(0).to(device)\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"

\\\"libero_10\\\": 520,\\n    \\\"libero_90\\\": 400,\\n}\\n\\n# --- Normalization helpers ---\\n\\ndef load_norm_stats(pretrained_dir, device):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Load mean/std normalization stats from checkpoint.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    from safetensors.torch import load_file\\n    pre_path = Path(pretrained_dir) / \\\"policy_preprocessor_step_5_normalizer_processor.safetensors\\\"\\n    post_path = Path(pretrained_dir) / \\\"policy_postprocessor_step_1_unnormalizer_processor.safetensors\\\"\\n    pre = load_file(str(pre_path))\\n    post = load_file(str(post_path))\\n    eps = 1e-8\\n    return {\\n        \\\"state_mean\\\": pre[\\\"observation.state.mean\\\"].to(device),\\n        \\\"state_std\\\": pre[\\\"observation.state.std\\\"].to(device),\\n        \\\"action_mean\\\": post[\\\"action.mean\\\"].to(device),\\n        \\\"action_std\\\": post[\\\"action.std\\\"].to(device),\\n        \\\"eps\\\": eps,\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef normalize_state(state_tensor, norm_stats):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Normalize observation state: (x - mean) / (std + eps).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    return (state_tensor - norm_stats[\\\"state_mean\\\"]) / (norm_stats[\\\"state_std\\\"] + norm_stats[\\\"eps\\\"])\\n\\n\\ndef unnormalize_actions(actions, norm_stats):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Unnormalize predicted actions: x * std + mean.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    return actions * norm_stats[\\\"action_std\\\"] + norm_stats[\\\"action_mean\\\"]\\n\\n\\ndef parse_args():\\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\\\"Eval official SmolVLA on LIBERO\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--pretrained_dir\\\", type=str, required=True,\\n                   help=\\\"Path to official lerobot-format checkpoint dir\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--suite\\\", type=str, default=\\\"libero_object\\\",\\n                   choices=list(SUITE_MAX_STEPS.keys()))\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--num_episodes\\\", type=int, default=50)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--gpu\\\", type=int, default=0)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--seed\\\", type=int, default=42)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--action_horizon\\\", type=int, default=50)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--max_steps\\\", type=int, default=None)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--output\\\", type=str, default=None)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--mode\\\", choices=[\\\"baseline\\\", \\\"random_prune\\\"], default=\\\"baseline\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--pruning_method\\\", type=str, default=\\\"true_removal\\\",\\n                   choices=[\\\"zero_mask\\\", \\\"true_removal\\\"])\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--k_ratio\\\", type=float, default=0.9,\\n                   help=\\\"Fraction of visual tokens to KEEP (0.9 = keep 90%%)\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--task_ids\\\", type=str, default=None)\\n    return p.parse_args()\\n\\n\\n# --- State preprocessing ---\\n\\ndef quat_to_axis_angle(quat: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:\\n    q = quat.astype(np.float64)\\n    w = np.clip(q[3], -1.0, 1.0)\\n    den = np.sqrt(max(1.0 - w * w, 0.0))\\n    if den > 1e-10:\\n        angle = 2.0 * np.arccos(w)\\n        axis = q[:3] / den\\n        return (axis * angle).astype(np.float32)\\n    return np.zeros(3, dtype=np.float32)\\n\\n\\ndef extract_state(obs: dict) -> np.ndarray:\\n    rs = obs[\\\"robot_state\\\"]\\n    eef_pos = np.asarray(rs[\\\"eef\\\"][\\\"pos\\\"], dtype=np.float32)\\n    eef_quat = np.asarray(rs[\\\"eef\\\"][\\\"quat\\\"], dtype=np.float64)\\n    gripper_qpos = np.asarray(rs[\\\"gripper\\\"][\\\"qpos\\\"], dtype=np.float32)\\n    eef_aa = quat_to_axis_angle(eef_quat)\\n    return np.concatenate([eef_pos, eef_aa, gripper_qpos])\\n\\n\\ndef obs_to_batch(obs, task_text, tokenizer, camera_keys, device, norm_stats=None):\\n    batch = {}\\n    for cam_key in camera_keys:\\n        short_name = cam_key.split(\\\".\\\")[-1]\\n        img = obs[\\\"pixels\\\"][short_name]\\n        img_t = torch.from_numpy(np.asarray(img)).permute(2, 0, 1).float() / 255.0\\n        img_t = img_t.unsqueeze(0)\\n        img_t = torch.flip(img_t, dims=[2, 3])\\n        batch[cam_key] = img_t.to(device)\\n\\n    state = extract_state(obs)\\n    state_t = torch.from_numpy(state).unsqueeze(0).to(device)\\n    if norm_stats is not None:\\n        state_t = normalize_state(state_t, norm_stats)\\n    batch[\\\"observation.state\\\"] = state_t\\n\\n    text = task_text if task_text.endswith(\\\"\\\\n\\\") else task_text + \\\"\\\\n\\\"\\n    encoded = tokenizer(\\n        [text], padding=\\\"longest\\\", max_length=48,\\n        truncation=True, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\",\\n    )\\n    batch[\\\"observation.language.tokens\\\"] = encoded[\\\"input_ids\\\"].to(device)\\n    batch[\\\"observation.language.attention_mask\\\"] = encoded[\\\"attention_mask\\\"].bool().to(device)\\n    return batch\\n\\n\\n# --- Model helpers ---\\n\\ndef find_connector(model):\\n    for name, module in model.named_modules():\\n        if name.endswith(\\\".connector\\\") or \\\"multi_modal_projector\\\" in name:\\n            log.info(\\\"Found connector: %s (%s)\\\", name, type(module).__name__)\\n            return module\\n    return None\\n\\n\\ndef register_random_prune_hook(connector, k_ratio, method=\\\"true_removal\\\"):\\n    def hook_fn(module, input, output):\\n        tokens = output[0] if isinstance(output, tuple) else output\\n        if tokens.dim() != 3:\\n            return output\\n        B, N, D = tokens.shape\\n        k = max(1, int(N * k_ratio))\\n        # Same random selection across batch for consistency\\n        scores = torch.rand(1, N, device=tokens.device).expand(B, -1)\\n        _, topk_idx = torch.topk(scores, k, dim=-1)\\n\\n        if method == \\\"true_removal\\\":\\n            kept = torch.zeros(N, dtype=torch.bool, device=tokens.device)\\n            kept[topk_idx[0]] = True\\n            masked = tokens[:, kept, :]\\n        else:\\n            mask = torch.zeros(B, N, device=tokens.device)\\n            mask.scatter_(1, topk_idx, 1.0)\\n            masked = tokens * mask.unsqueeze(-1)\\n\\n        if isinstance(output, tuple):\\n            return (masked,) + output[1:]\\n        return masked\\n    return connector.register_forward_hook(hook_fn)\\n\\n\\n@torch.no_grad()\\ndef predict_actions(policy, batch):\\n    images, img_masks = policy.prepare_images(batch)\\n    state = policy.prepare_state(batch)\\n    lang_tokens = batch[\\\"observation.language.tokens\\\"]\\n    lang_masks = batch[\\\"observation.language.attention_mask\\\"]\\n    actions = policy.model.sample_actions(\\n        images, img_masks, lang_tokens, lang_masks, state\\n    )\\n    original_action_dim = policy.config.action_feature.shape[0]\\n    return actions[0, :, :original_action_dim]\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"}

init_states.shape[0]\n            obs = env.set_init_state(init_states[init_state_id])\n\n            action_chunk = None\n            action_idx = 0\n            success = False\n\n            for step in range(max_steps):\n                if action_chunk is None or action_idx >= args.action_horizon:\n                    batch = obs_to_batch(obs, task_desc, tokenizer, camera_keys, device, norm_stats)\n\n                    if args.mode == \"random_prune\":\n                        hook = register_random_prune_hook(\n                            connector, args.k_ratio, args.pruning_method)\n                        action_chunk = predict_actions(policy, batch)\n                        hook.remove()\n                        action_chunk = unnormalize_actions(action_chunk, norm_stats)\n                    else:\n                        action_chunk = predict_actions(policy, batch)\n                        action_chunk = unnormalize_actions(action_chunk, norm_stats)\n\n                    action_idx = 0\n\n                action = action_chunk[action_idx].cpu().numpy().astype(np.float32)\n                action = np.clip(action, -1.0, 1.0)\n                action_idx += 1\n\n                obs, reward, done, info = env.step(action)\n\n                if done or reward > 0.5:\n                    success = True\n                    break\n\n            ep_steps = step + 1\n            task_successes.append(success)\n            task_lengths.append(ep_steps)\n            log.info(\"  Ep %d/%d | %s | steps=%d\",\n                     ep + 1, args.num_episodes,\n                     \"SUCCESS\" if success else \"FAIL\", ep_steps)\n\n            env.close()\n\n        n_succ = sum(task_successes)\n        sr = n_succ / args.num_episodes if args.num_episodes > 0 else 0.0\n        all_task_results[task_id] = {\n            \"task_id\": task_id,\n            \"task_description\": task_desc,\n            \"success_count\": n_succ,\n            \"total_episodes\": args.num_episodes,\n            \"success_rate\": round(sr, 4),\n            \"mean_steps\": round(float(np.mean(task_lengths)), 1),\n        }\n        total_successes += n_succ\n        total_episodes += args.num_episodes\n        log.info(\"  Task %d SR: %d/%d (%.1f%%)\", task_id, n_succ, args.num_episodes, sr * 100)\n\n    overall_sr = total_successes / total_episodes if total_episodes > 0 else 0.0\n    _, ci_lo, ci_hi = wilson_ci(total_successes, total_episodes)\n\n    log.info(\"=\" * 60)\n    log.info(\"SUITE: %s | MODE: %s\", args.suite, args.mode)\n    if args.mode == \"random_prune\":\n        log.info(\"PRUNING: %s | k_ratio: %.2f\", args.pruning_method, args.k_ratio)\n    log.info(\"Overall SR: %d/%d (%.1f%%)\", total_successes, total_episodes, overall_sr * 100)\n    log.info(\"95%% Wilson CI: [%.1f%%, %.1f%%]\", ci_lo * 100, ci_hi * 100)\n    for tid, tr in sorted(all_task_results.items()):\n        log.info(\"  Task %d: %.0f%% (%d/%d) -- %s\",\n                 tid, tr[\"success_rate\"] * 100, tr[\"success_count\"],\n                 tr[\"total_episodes\"], tr[\"task_description\"])\n    log.info(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    summary = {\n        \"suite\": args.suite,\n        \"mode\": args.mode,\n        \"pruning_method\": args.pruning_method if args.mode == \"random_prune\" else \"none\",\n        \"k_ratio\": args.k_ratio if args.mode == \"random_prune\" else None,\n        \"pretrained_dir\": args.pretrained_dir,\n        \"total_episodes\": total_episodes,\n        \"total_successes\": total_successes,\n        \"overall_success_rate\": round(overall_sr, 4),\n        \"wilson_ci_95\": [round(ci_lo, 4), round(ci_hi, 4)],\n        \"num_episodes_per_task\": args.num_episodes,\n        \"max_steps\": max_steps,\n        \"action_horizon\": args.action_horizon,\n        \"seed\": args.seed,\n        \"per_task\": all_task_results,\n    }\n    output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    with open(output_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(summary, f, indent=2)\n    log.info(\"Results saved to %s\", output_path)\n\n    return summary\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\nPYFILE_EOF"
}
```
